{"forms":[{"form":"stakes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"We have surveyor's stakes at all four corners of this field, to mark exactly its borders.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A piece of wood or other material, usually long and slender, pointed at one end so as to be easily driven into the ground as a marker or a support or stay."]},{"glosses":["A piece of wood driven in the ground, placed in the middle of the court, that is used as the finishing point after scoring 12 hoops in croquet."],"qualifier":"croquet"},{"glosses":["A stick or similar object (e.g., steel channel or angle stock) inserted upright in a lop, eye, or mortise, at the side or end of a cart, flat car, flatbed trailer, or the like, to prevent goods from falling off; often connected in a grid forming a stakebody."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Thomas Cranmer was burnt at the stake.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The piece of timber to which a person condemned to death was affixed to be burned."],"tags":["with-definite-article"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The owners let the managers eventually earn a stake in the business.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A share or interest in a business or a given situation."]},{"glosses":["That which is laid down as a wager; that which is staked or hazarded; a pledge."]},{"glosses":["A small anvil usually furnished with a tang to enter a hole in a bench top, as used by tinsmiths, blacksmiths, etc., for light work, punching hole in or cutting a work piece, or for specific forming techniques etc."]},{"glosses":["A territorial division comprising all the Mormons (typically several thousand) in a geographical area."],"tags":["Mormonism"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/steɪk/"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"croquet","word":"peg"}],"upstream_line":209258,"upstream_offset":1009152356,"upstream_record_sha256":"67b8b1bb31b29f6317f7e98bb78f86176683015e8d9535dfcccec9ba0f3b908e","word":"stake"}
{"forms":[{"form":"stakes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"staking","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"staked","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"staked","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"to stake vines or plants","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To fasten, support, defend, or delineate with stakes."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To pierce or wound with a stake."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To put at risk upon success in competition, or upon a future contingency."],"synonyms":[{"word":"bet"},{"word":"hazard"},{"word":"wager"}],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"John went broke, so to keep him playing, Jill had to stake him.","type":"example"},{"text":"His family staked him $10,000 to get his business started.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To provide (another) with money in order to engage in an activity as betting or a business venture."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"glosses":["To deposit and risk a considerable amount of cryptocurrency in order to participate in the proof of stake process of verification."],"topics":["business","cryptocurrencies","cryptocurrency","finance"]},{"glosses":["To stretch leather skin over a piece of iron held knee-height, in preparation to use the skin in the creation of gloves."],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/steɪk/"}],"upstream_line":209259,"upstream_offset":1009190793,"upstream_record_sha256":"ea1c44566c732df541222217bc98e5d33b5388fbafd84e76f739fd95ebfa8a4d","word":"stake"}
